Looks

looks.md

Read on 2026-10-18 from run 20261018-003441-640-4813 (feature pickletools-look-catalogue, commit d4f4e67 plus the map of 83d1d3e; Tests/Pickle/Evidence/look-catalogue), with the mods of wsl-deps.look-catalogue.map: vanilla and DLCs (Royalty, Ideology, Biotech, Anomaly, Odyssey), the Sanctuary body and facial animation mods. A mod missing from the list lacks its names. Names are the defNames the look steps take (Nelim's Pickle Tools: "<pawn>" hairstyle is "<name>", and the matching beard, head type and tattoo steps of ColonistRace; the body type has no look step yet (the list is for choosing a pawn body, e.g. through a ColonistRace body step if one is added)). A hairstyle may carry a gender in the log; a head type too; a tattoo its type (Face or Body). Second run 2026-10-18 05:36 (ticket 1d58, Tests/Pickle/Evidence/look-catalogue-mods-2, passed 1/1) added Roo's HD Hairstyles (RBAHD_ and RBDHD_ prefixes), Vanilla Hair Expanded (VHE_), Tattoos Expanded and Tribal Tattoos (STribalTat_) to the hairstyle and tattoo tables. Roo's RBAHD_ values are hair with an accessory (headbands, bandanas, hijab, turbans): mind the hat rule of GALERIE.md (SanctuaryBacklot). Photographs of each value: not taken yet.

Colours

colours.md

Read on 2026-10-18 from run 20261018-003441-640-4813 (feature pickletools-look-catalogue, commit d4f4e67 plus the map of 83d1d3e; Tests/Pickle/Evidence/look-catalogue), with the mods of wsl-deps.look-catalogue.map: vanilla and DLCs (Royalty, Ideology, Biotech, Anomaly, Odyssey), the Sanctuary body and facial animation mods. A mod missing from the list lacks its names. Every ColorDef of the game by mod and type, each followed by its colour as hex (RGB). Hair: the hair dye colours; Ideo: the ideology garment and style colours; Structure: floor and wall paints (the "painted" step of StageDecor); Weapon: Odyssey weapon tints; Misc and Planning: other uses. A colour may also be given as free rgb, as the steps hair colour is rgb (r, g, b) and dyed rgb (r, g, b) do. Photographs of each value: not taken yet.

Utility items

items.md

Read on 2026-10-18 from run 6e69 (feature pickletools-item-catalogue, step the utility item options are listed, map wsl-deps.look-catalogue.map; Tests/Pickle/Evidence/item-catalogue): vanilla and the DLCs (Royalty, Ideology, Biotech, Anomaly, Odyssey) with the Sanctuary body and facial animation mods. Items that are neither weapons nor apparel, not corpses, not chunks, one table row per mod and item category; total 461. The step takes no filter: some rows are raw materials, drugs or implants rather than props. Photographs: not taken yet (no scenario places them).

Names are defNames (the carry steps and StageDecor take a defName: "<pawn>" carries the item "<defName>", I place the decor "<defName>" at (x, z)).

Face parts

face-parts.md

Read on 2026-10-09 from run ff33 (PickleTools/evidence/face-list3, step Nelim's Pickle Tools: the facial animations are listed), with the standard mod list of the Sanctuary map (wsl-deps.sanctuary.map). A mod list that lacks a mod lacks its names: the step fails and prints the valid ones. Photos of each value: not taken yet (planned, one contact sheet per part).

Steps (all start with Nelim's Pickle Tools: ): "<pawn>" mouth is "<name>", brows are, lids are, face skin is, eyeballs are, face head shape is, and the whole-face "<pawn>" face kit is "<kit>".

Photographs (2026-10-09, runs 90ae, 58ce, 504a; Tests/Pickle/.../pickletools-face-parts-gallery.feature). One photograph per value, in the order of the tables, on Nelim of the save Nelims-tribe at 20 degrees. Each scenario starts from the kit neutral and sets one part value after another, so a photograph also shows the parts set earlier in the same scenario (the cheek mark of a skin stays on the later eyeball photographs): read the changed part, not the whole face. Sheets: docs/Make-PartSheets.ps1.

Kits (face kit is)

Fixed list in the code (ColonistRace/Source/FaceSteps.cs). Each kit sets its parts together; a part step run afterwards overrides one part. A kit fails, naming the part, if a def is missing from the loaded mods.

KitMouthLidsBrowsSkinLook on the photo (run 47e8)
smileMouthSmileLidCheerfulBrowNormalSkinRosyCheeksSlight smile, flirty lids.
calmMouthLipsTinySmileLidSimpleBrowNormalSkinNormalSmall red lips, round eyes.
sadMouthSadLidUnimpressedBrowRaisedSkinNormalHalf lids, flat mouth.
angryMouthScowlLidHardenedBrowTriangleSkinNormalFrown, angled brows.
smugMouthSmugLidFlirtyBrowNormalSkinNormalA cat mouth.
neutralMouthSimpleMouthLidSimpleBrowNormalSkinNormalA flat line.

Every kit also resets the rest of the face to its neutral value (EyeNormal, HeadNormal), so no part of a previous kit stays on the pawn.

The kits use names of Vanilla Textures Expanded - [NL] Facial Animation (mouths, skin), of [NL] Facial Animation - Experimentals (brows, head shape) and of the core (lids, eyeballs). On a mod list without that mod, they fail.

Heat

A kit or part step leaves the heat face (sweat, blush) of the room. To photograph a clean face, follow the parts or kit with facial expression is "normal" (proved on a photo, run 4a58); see FACIAL-ANIMATIONS.md for the 55 animations.

mods-looks.md

Read on 2026-10-18 with scripts/Search-Workshop.sh (default mode, ripgrep, -i '*.xml'), pattern <(TattooDef|HairDef|BeardDef|HeadTypeDef)[ >]: 9547 of 9548 folders of the Workshop and the local Mods folder held an XML file and were read (the other one holds none). Hits Abstract="True" and commented lines are left out. 457 mods declare at least one.

Counts are text hits, not loaded defs. A mod that keeps a def in several version folders (1.4, 1.5, 1.6) counts it once per folder, and a def that LoadFolders never activates counts too (SEARCHING.md: "A defName found is not a def loaded"). Read the mod before choosing it; the catalogue run (pickletools-look-catalogue) gives the real defNames of the mods that are staged. Clothing and weapons are not in this list (their pattern is too broad: a second search).

Mods: gear

mods-gear.md

Read on 2026-10-18 with scripts/Search-Workshop.sh (default mode, ripgrep, -i '*.xml'), pattern <(apparel|weaponTags)>: 9547 of 9548 folders read (the other one holds no XML). An <apparel> block marks a garment def, a <weaponTags> list marks a weapon def. Commented lines are left out. Only mods with at least 3 hits are listed.

Counts are text hits, not loaded defs: a def kept in several version folders counts once per folder, and one that LoadFolders never activates counts too (SEARCHING.md). Animal apparel mods are among the garments (their defs have an <apparel> block too). The catalogue run gives the real defNames of the mods that are staged.

Mods: world

mods-world.md

Read on 2026-10-18 with scripts/Search-Workshop.sh (default mode, ripgrep, -i '*.xml'), pattern <(wildness|plant|designationCategory)>: 9547 of 9548 folders read (the other one holds no XML). A <designationCategory> line marks a building the player can place from an Architect tab (its value is the tab: Furniture, Structure, Production, Misc, ...; furniture and props are told apart by it), a <wildness> line an animal, an empty <plant> block a plant. Commented lines are left out. Only mods with at least 3 hits per list are shown.

Counts are text hits, not loaded defs: a def kept in several version folders counts once per folder, and one that LoadFolders never activates counts too (SEARCHING.md). A building that is only reachable through a research or a mod setting counts as well. The exact buildings of the mods that are staged, with size and research, come from the catalogue run (pickletools-building-catalogue, CATALOGUE-BUILDINGS.md).

Seren

seren.md

Read 2026-10-25 from the mod folder (Workshop 3726565301, packageId odysseylotus.serenbiotech, supports 1.6 and 1.7; 1.6 and 1.7 hold the same defs). Names only: this catalogue is what the XML defines, not a photograph. Colour, look and ear genes (hair, skin, markings, sensors, ears, eyes, body masks) are in genes.md; this file lists the rest and, above all, the face parts, which are loaded by a compatibility folder and not by the main defs.

Mod folder: C:/Program Files (x86)/Steam/steamapps/workshop/content/294100/3726565301.

Which face set is loaded

LoadFolders.xml picks one face-part set by active mods. Defs of a set that is not loaded do not exist: asking the step for one of them fails.

Set (folder under 1.6/Compatibility/)Loaded when
Facial Animations - ExperimentalsNals.FacialAnimation and Nals.FacialAnimationExperimentals active. This is the set of the NSB map wsl-deps.sanctuary-seren.map.
Facial Animations - Vanilla Textures ExpandedNals.FacialAnimation and VanillaExpanded.VTEXE.FacialAnims active, Experimentals not active
Facial Animations - NiceFAAndromeda.FacialAnimation (or .ALT) active

All face parts target the gene OL_SerenEyes (skin parts target a marking gene): a pawn without OL_SerenEyes does not get them.

Experimentals set (the one NSB loads)

PartdefNamesNotes
Head (3)SerenHeadNormal, SerenHeadPointy, SerenHeadSquaretexPath Things/Pawn/Seren/Heads_Blank/<Name>
Lids (7)SerenLidNormal, SerenLidGeek, SerenLidPointy, SerenLidFlashy, SerenLidQuite, SerenLidSleepy, SerenLidSharpSerenLidGeek is gender Female only. Quite is the spelling in the def
Eyeballs (4)SerenEyesNormal, SerenEyesDull, SerenEyesIntense, SerenEyesEmptyprobability 1 (Normal), 0.25, 0.25, 0.1
Brows (2)SerenBrowNormal, SerenBrowThick
Mouth (2)SerenMouthNormal, SerenMouthHappySerenMouthHappy has probability 0: never random, set it by name only
Emotion (1)SerenEmotionNormalan EmotionTypeDef, not a face part of the Nelim step
Skin (7)SerenBrows, SerenCenterLarge, SerenCenterSmall, SerenCheeks, SerenFreckles, SerenTarget, SerenTriStareach targets one marking gene: SerenMarkings_Brows, _Big, _Small, _Cheeks, _Freckles, _Target, _Tristar

The patch of this set removes the renderNodeProperties of OL_SerenEyes and of the 7 marking genes (the face parts draw them instead) and swaps the ear texture to Things/Seren/Ears/FacialAnimations/SerenEars.

Names NSB failed on: SerenLidCheerful and SerenLidSimple

They do not exist in any set. Nearest names:

AskedSetReal defNameTexture
SerenLidCheerfulVanilla Textures Expanded onlyOL_SerenLidCheerfulFAThings/Pawn/Seren/Lids/Cheerful
SerenLidSimpleVanilla Textures Expanded onlyOL_SerenLidSimpleFAThings/Pawn/Seren/Lids/Simple

These two are not loaded with Experimentals. To have a cheerful or simple Seren lid on the NSB map, either load the VTE facial animation set (VanillaExpanded.VTEXE.FacialAnims, and then Experimentals must be off: the folders are exclusive), or pick an Experimentals lid: for a cheerful look SerenLidFlashy or SerenLidNormal, for a plain one SerenLidNormal. Only a photograph of each tells which fits; none is made yet.

Vanilla Textures Expanded set (not loaded by NSB)

The VTE set builds its parts inside one patch (it needs the mod Vanilla Textures Expanded - [NL] Facial Animation), and keeps the vanilla head types.

PartdefNames

Xenotype

One: OL_SerenBase (label "seren"). Genes: OL_SerenPhysiology, OL_SerenBlood, OL_SerenEyes, OL_SerenEars, OL_DarkVision, OL_ElongatedFingers, Hair_Grayless, Beard_NoBeardOnly (Ideology), Body_Thin, Body_Standard, Libido_Low, and the pools above (13 skin, 19 hair, 8 sensors, 7 markings, 6 body masks). Name makers: NamerSerenMale, NamerSerenFemale (name banks SerenFirstMale, SerenFirstFemale, SerenLast, SerenDeity in Common/Languages/English/Strings/NameBanks/).

What to do for the looks of Ada and Koji

1. The face is picked by name per part: head, lids, eyes, brows, mouth and skin come from the Experimentals table above. Every name there exists on the NSB map.

2. A face part only draws on a pawn that has the gene OL_SerenEyes; a skin part only with its marking gene.

3. The photographs of the Experimentals face parts are in the table above (filed, not yet read).

Not sheeted yet

  • Utility items: values in items.md (run 6e69), no photographs by design.
  • Buildings: one photograph per Architect tab in img/looks/ (blocks 31xx) (4 of 14 tabs so far: Joy, Misc, Security, Ship).
  • Animals: portraits not photographed yet (ticket 5abb to refile).

Gaps

gaps.md

Method: the 235 steps of docs/steps.md (353 steps of Nelim's Pickle Tools) that take a {string}, grouped by what the string names, against the catalogues in ColonistRace/docs/ and docs/SANCTUAIRE-LIEUX.md. Pickle's own 238 steps were not read (their catalogue is Docs/steps.md of RimWorks/Rimworld-Pickle).

Rule (owner, 2026-10-25): a catalogue is only for what is visual (one photograph per value). Lists that nobody looks at (thoughts, sounds, research, ideologies, scenarios, storytellers, difficulties, traders, stats, jobs, interface keys) get no catalogue: the step fails and lists the valid names when a value is wrong, which is enough.

Have

Hairstyles, beards, head types, face and body tattoos (Roo's two mods added 2026-10-25), body types, garments, hats, colours, eye and skin colour genes, face parts and expressions, weapons and crafts, utility items, buildings, animals and plants by mod, the Sanctuary places.

Not a catalogue of its own

  • Floors (terrain): seen through the Architect menu, like the buildings (one photograph per Architect category, ArchitectStudio owns the menu steps).
  • Joy givers and instruments: buildings of the Architect menu (Joy category), same catalogue.

What to build next

The listing steps are cheap (one line per mod, like LookCatalogueSteps): xenotypes, genes, pawn kinds and the studio presets are the four to write after the weather. Owner to choose the order; only the weather listing is built.
